CobbleEffort: Digital @VERSION@ (Minecraft 1.21.1, Cobblemon 1.8.4)
=====================================================================

A Digimon crossover for Cobblemon. Since 0.2.0 the Digimon themselves live here (they used to ship inside
CobbleEffort; their ids are unchanged, so Digimon you already caught stay as they are). They spawn, battle, level up
and evolve exactly like Pokémon, on hand-built models, and are added in wikimon.net's alphabetical order a batch at a
time; wikimon decides who evolves into whom (when several evolutions are possible at once, Cobblemon offers them all
and you pick). Sizes: Adult 15 blocks tall, Perfect 25, Ultimate 35, Super Ultimate 45.

* Digiballs: the Digiball is three times as good as a Poké Ball on any Digimon below Ultimate (craft one from a
  Poké Ball, redstone, lapis lazuli and a copper ingot, or four from a Great Ball ringed with redstone, copper and
  lapis). Its tiers are four times as good on their own stages: the Wireframe Ball on In-Training and Rookie
  Digimon (Poké Ball + glass pane + redstone), the Data-Net Ball on Champions (Great Ball + one Vaccine, Data and
  Virus Fragment + string, makes 2) and the Chrome Digizoid Ball on Ultimates and Megas (Ultra Ball + Chrome Digizoid
  Ingot, makes 2). Wild Ultimate and Mega Digimon (Perfect, Ultimate and Super Ultimate) are too much data for any
  other ball: only a Chrome Digizoid Ball (or a Master Ball) can catch them.
* Digitama (Digi-Egg): keep one in your inventory and it warms up (the bar under it fills over 5 minutes). When it
  starts to shine, use it to hatch a random baby Digimon (Baby I) at level 1, straight into your party (or your PC).
  Find them in dungeon, mineshaft, temple, ancient city and village chests; wild Digimon leave one behind 5% of the
  times you defeat them.

Attributes (Vaccine, Data, Virus, Free, Variable)
  Every Digimon has an attribute, and in battle they play rock-paper-scissors: Vaccine beats Virus, Virus beats Data,
  Data beats Vaccine. Hitting the attribute you beat does 1.25x damage, hitting the one that beats you 0.8x (Free and
  Variable are neutral). Defeated wild Digimon drop Vaccine, Data or Virus Fragments (their own attribute; more from
  higher stages). Use fragments on your own Digimon: 6 of one kind in a row rewrite its attribute to that kind. A
  Vaccine, Data or Virus Chip (8 fragments round a gold ingot) does it at once. A Digimon's attribute also opens
  digivolutions: a Vaccine, Data or Virus Digimon's route into one of the other two needs that attribute (Free and
  Variable Digimon take any route; the Digivice scan shows what each route needs).

Field buffs
  Digimon belong to fields (Nature Spirits, Deep Savers, Nightmare Soldiers, Metal Empire, Wind Guardians, Dragon's
  Roar, Virus Busters, Jungle Troopers, Dark Area, Saiyu Warriors, Toho Braves). Fight where your Digimon's field is in
  its element and it does 1.15x damage: Nature Spirits in forests and plains, Deep Savers near water, Nightmare
  Soldiers at night or underground, Metal Empire (and any Electric Digimon) in thunderstorms, Wind Guardians on
  mountains and high up, Dragon's Roar in deserts, badlands, savannas and the Nether, Virus Busters by day under open
  sky, Jungle Troopers in jungles, swamps and bamboo, Dark Area in the Nether and the deep dark, Saiyu Warriors in
  savannas and deserts, Toho Braves in cherry groves and bamboo.

The Tamer's Digivice (iron, glass pane, redstone, a Chrome Digizoid Ingot and a stone button)
  * Use it on any Digimon to scan it: stage, attribute, types, what it digivolves into and what that takes (your own
    Digimon show green for what's met, red for what isn't), and what it comes from.
  * Use it in the air for your Tamer Energy and your party's attributes; sneak and use it to open your PC anywhere.
  * In battle, use it on your battling Digimon to warp-digivolve it for the rest of the battle: one stage up for 40
    Tamer Energy, two stages while sneaking for 80. It takes the new form's stats and types and looks the part, and
    goes back to normal when the battle ends. Once per battle per Digimon; it takes your turn.
  * Jogress: with a DNA partner at hand (in your party, healthy, or battling beside it), a plain use DNA-digivolves
    instead (60 energy): XV-mon + Stingmon into Paildramon, War Greymon + Metal Garurumon into Omegamon, and every
    other pair wikimon lists between Digimon in the mod.
  * Tamer Energy (0-100) lives on the Digivice and refills by itself, a point every 6 seconds while you carry it.

Digi-Modify cards (craft 2 from paper, a fragment and an item; also found in dungeon, mineshaft, temple, stronghold
library, ancient city and buried-treasure chests)
  In battle, with a Digivice anywhere in your inventory, use a card on your battling Digimon (it takes your turn):
  High-Speed Plug-In (Speed +2), Metal Armor (Defense +2, Sp. Def +1), Giga Destroyer (Attack and Sp. Atk +1, critical
  hits more often), Recovery Disk (heals half, cures its status), Dot Virus (the foe turns into tiny 8-bit dot data for
  3 turns: half Attack, no special moves) and Bug Card (3 turns: the attribute triangle is inverted for the foe).

Digi-Eggs (Digimentals): the Digi-Egg of Courage and the Digi-Egg of Friendship armor-digivolve a V-mon into
  Fladramon or Lighdramon (use the egg on it).

The Digi-Meat farm
  Digi-Meat grows out of the ground like carrots (plant the meat itself on farmland). Wild Digimon sometimes drop one,
  village chests hold some, or craft one from raw beef and bone meal. A ripe crop gives 1-4 meat and now and then a
  Sirloin. Super Carrots: a golden carrot and a Data Fragment, then plant them. All three are food for you too, and fed
  to your own Pokémon (use it on them) they fill them up and raise friendship: Sirloin also +10 HP and Attack EVs,
  Super Carrot +10 Speed and Sp. Def EVs.

The training gym
  Punching Bag (Attack), Treadmill (Speed), Training Waterfall (Defense), Meditation Mat (Sp. Def) and Target Dummy
  (Sp. Atk). Use one: a marker sweeps along a bar above your hotbar; use the block again while it's in the green (gold
  is perfect). Your sent-out Pokémon nearest the block trains (else the first healthy one in your party): perfect = 8
  EVs, good = 4, a miss = 1.

Chrome Digizoid: the toughest ore, deep underground (below y 8, a little rarer than diamond; iron pickaxe or
  better). Smelt the raw ore into ingots (9 make a block). It goes into the Digivice and Chrome Digizoid Balls.

The Digital World
  A dimension of its own: File Island's wireframe plains (glowing wire cubes, cyan data streams), the Voxel Forest's
  giant 8-bit trees, the Server Continent's circuit-board flats, server racks and plug-in socket cliffs, and the Dark
  Area, a corrupted black-and-violet land where Virus Digimon, Pico Devimon and Bakemon gather. Only Digimon live
  there (every Digimon below Ultimate spawns in the biome that suits its type). Its water is data: standing in it
  refills your Digivice's Tamer Energy and heals your sent-out Pokémon.
  * The way in: an Ancient Terminal, used holding a Digivice. They stand in Abandoned Server Terminals (ruined server
    rooms in the overworld and the Digital World, with loot: fragments, chips, Digi-Cores, cards, Digi-Eggs, Chrome
    Digizoid, and an abandoned Digitama cradle to rescue an egg from), or craft one (smooth stone, a glass pane and a
    Digi-Core). You arrive on a circuit pad with a terminal for the way home, which takes you back where you left.
  * Digital Rifts: now and then a rift tears open near someone for three minutes; walk into it to be digitized.
  * Primary Village (File Island): rows of Digitama cradles watched over by Elecmon. Trade 4 Digi-Meat for an egg
    (use the meat on a cradle); empty cradles lay new eggs in time.
  * Dark Towers (overworld and Dark Area): obsidian spires banded with black glass. Within 64 blocks of a standing
    tower's core no Digimon digivolves past Child (no level-up digivolutions offered, no Digivice warps). Climb the
    ladder to the top chamber and break the core (diamond pickaxe): its guardian, a strong Virus Digimon, challenges
    you. Beat it for Chrome Digizoid, Virus data and maybe a Dark Spore.
  * Dark Spore: use it on your Digimon to rewrite it to Virus and force a Virus digivolution at once.
  * The black market: at night, underground or in the Dark Area a shady Vademon or Etemon sometimes turns up for ten
    minutes. Use it to trade Dark Spores, "stolen" Digitama, Virus Chips, Chrome Digizoid and glitch cards for
    emeralds or Virus Fragments.

Crests and the Tag
  Raid bosses and server terminals give Crests: Courage, Friendship, Love, Knowledge, Sincerity, Reliability, Hope and
  Light. Socket one in a Tag (two chains and a Chrome Digizoid Ingot; hold the Tag in your other hand and use the
  Crest) and wear it in the Accessories necklace slot (or just carry it). It charges as you live by it: Courage when
  you win with a Pokémon down to a quarter of its HP, Friendship as you feed and train, Love when eggs and Digitama
  hatch, Knowledge as you scan new species and craft chips and cards, Sincerity as you harvest ripe crops,
  Reliability when you trade or win beside other players, Hope when you win with one Pokémon left, Light when you win
  by day. At 100% the Crest unlocks Ultimate and Mega: use the Tag on your Digimon to digivolve it a stage at once,
  or sneak-warp with the Digivice for free (from Adult, two stages, to Ultimate). A Digimon holding a Crest pays half
  for warps.

Raids
  Every two or three hours (with players online) a rift opens near someone and a Demon Lord or Royal Knight comes
  through: a level 80 boss in three phases, with a boss bar for everyone near. Battle it one after another; everyone
  who fought it shares the reward (Chrome Digizoid, a Crest, a Digi-Core, maybe a Tag). While it's loose the land round
  the rift turns to wireframe and circuitry; it all comes back when the raid ends (gamerule digitalDigitization).
  Operators: /digital raid start [player], /digital raid stop, /digital rift, /digital world.

Digimon attacks in the world
  In battle, a Digimon's fire attacks burst into flame where they land, its missiles and cannons (Giga Destroyer,
  Garuru Cannon...) explode and its water attacks splash and put out fires. With the gamerule digitalMoveGriefing on
  (off by default) they change the world too: fires catch, sand turns to glass, blasts break blocks, water leaves
  puddles that drain away.

Digizoid gear (above Netherite)
  Smithing: a Netherite piece + a coloured Digizoid Ingot + the Digizoid Upgrade (diamonds round a Chrome Digizoid
  Ingot and a Netherite Upgrade; copy it with diamonds and a Chrome Digizoid block). Coloured ingots: a Chrome
  Digizoid Ingot with a redstone block (Red), a lapis block (Blue), a gold block (Gold), or obsidian and an ender
  pearl (Black). Red: the heaviest armor and knockback resistance, the hardest-hitting sword. Blue: each piece +4%
  speed, a quick sword. Gold: two or more pieces reflect a third of projectiles and take a quarter less from magic and
  blasts. Black: with the full set, sneak while sprinting to phase 6 blocks ahead; the sword blinks you where you
  look (right-click). Both on a cooldown.

The Digi-Farm
  Put your PC Pokémon out to pasture with Cobblemon's own Pasture block, then place a Digi-Farm Terminal (iron, a
  glass pane, hay and a Digi-Core) within 12 blocks. Every minute each tethered Pokémon works, depending on the farm
  goods within 6 blocks of the terminal: training gym blocks (EXP and EVs in that stat), bookshelves (EXP), Digi-Meat
  crops or composters (Digi-Meat), circuit blocks or server casings (attribute fragments, Digimon only), chests or
  barrels (things they scavenge). Collect it all from the terminal.

Partner jobs (sneak and use an empty hand on your own sent-out Digimon)
  * Storage: give an Adult-or-bigger Digimon a Chest Harness (leather round a chest) and it carries a double chest.
  * Forge: a Fire Digimon opens a fuel-free blast furnace, your own, which keeps smelting while you're away.
  * Mining: with a Ground, Rock or Steel Digimon out within 6 blocks, the stone and ore you mine with a pickaxe also
    takes the blocks behind and beside it (a 3-wide tunnel; broken as you, so claims still apply).

Tamers and the Colosseum
  Wandering Tamers turn up in villages with themed teams (a Crack Team officer with machines, Nature Spirits, Deep
  Savers, Nightmare Soldiers, Dragon's Roar, Virus Busters) at your party's level; talk to one to battle. The
  Colosseum (plains, savannas, deserts, meadows) keeps three Tamers: 1v1, 2v2 (doubles) and 3v3. Wins earn Tamer
  Rank points (Colosseum Tamers 25, once a day each; others 10) and Digi-Modify cards. Ranks: Rookie, Champion (100),
  Ultimate (300), Mega (700). /digital rank shows yours.

Spirit Evolution
  Twenty Spirits, a Human and a Beast Spirit for each of ten elements (Flame: Agnimon/Vritramon, Light: Wolfmon/
  Garummon, Ice: Chackmon/Blizzarmon, Wind: Fairimon/Shutumon, Thunder: Blitzmon/Bolgmon, Darkness: Duskmon/Velgrmon,
  Earth: Grottomon/Gigasmon, Wood: Arbormon/Petaldramon, Water: Ranamon/Calamaramon, Steel: Mercurymon/Sephirothmon).
  Find them in Spirit Temples, small shrines of each element's stone with a chest on a pedestal (Flame in deserts and
  badlands, Light in meadows and cherry groves, Ice in snowy lands, Wind in windswept hills, Thunder in savannas and
  plains, Darkness in dark forests, Earth in wooded/eroded badlands and stony shores, Wood in jungles and forests,
  Water on beaches and in swamps, Steel in taigas; the Digital World has them too), or craft them: a Digi-Core ringed
  by gold and the element's item for a Human Spirit, by Chrome Digizoid and the element's block for a Beast Spirit.
  Use a Spirit and you become its Digimon: you look the part, your hotbar becomes the spirit's power (right-click),
  a Spirit Dash and Release the Spirit, and you get the element's gifts (flight for Wind and Light, fire resistance
  for Flame, regeneration for Wood, water breathing for Water...). Human Spirits last 60 seconds and add speed and
  jumps; Beast Spirits last 40 seconds and hit harder (Strength II, absorption). The gauge shows above the hotbar.
  Your hotbar comes back when the spirit leaves (it's saved with the world, so a logout or a crash can't lose it; die
  as a spirit and it drops like the rest of your things), then the spirit rests for five minutes. Not in battles.

Bio-Merge
  Sneak and use the Digivice on your own sent-out Digimon when its friendship is at the maximum (255): you merge with
  it into its Mega (the furthest digivolution open to it) for 90 seconds, for 50 Tamer Energy. You keep your armor,
  fly, hit hard and attack with its element (from its type); your partner rests in its ball meanwhile.

Install: Fabric or NeoForge with Architectury API and Cobblemon 1.8.4. Shared as a main jar plus texture jars
("CobbleEffort: Digital Textures N"): install all of them.
